THE CITY · Rich and poor in coins and food: gpt-oss
Coins and food together, with gpt-oss. Every rich founder lives and they make 82 children. Twenty of the 50 poor founders survive, twice as many as with food or coins alone.
100 founders · 400 days · no government · seed 1 · gptoss120
